### [1] [ELECTRIC: Evidential Learning-Enhanced CT Reconstruction via Iterative Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00060)
*Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ELECTRIC提出了一种物理引导的贝叶斯低剂量CT重建框架，通过证据神经网络提供图像提议和误差预测性认知不确定性代理，将其转化为自适应精度场并嵌入泊松加权MAP更新中，形成一个图像-证据-精度-重建的闭环迭代过程。在AAPM Mayo低剂量CT数据集上，该方法相比滤波反投影将重建误差降低约70%，且对先验强度误设更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT重建中，传统方法难以有效结合物理模型与学习到的先验知识，且现有方法缺乏对重建不确定性的量化和对先验置信度的自适应处理。作者旨在通过证据学习框架将认知不确定性显式建模为迭代重建的状态变量，从而同时提升重建精度与可靠性。

Method: 提出ELECTRIC框架：1）证据神经网络（基于Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布）同时输出图像提议与认知不确定性代理；2）将不确定性转化为自适应精度场（precision field）；3）将精度场嵌入带泊松权重的最大后验（MAP）更新中，形成图像-证据-精度-重建的闭环迭代；4）先验置信度被视为迭代重建中的可学习状态变量，通过物理引导更新恢复测量一致性。

Result: 在AAPM Mayo低剂量CT数据集的留存患者上：1）学习到的先验均值相比滤波反投影减少约70%的重建误差；2）学习到的认知不确定性与误差具有预测相关性，支持选择性信任机制；3）物理引导更新恢复测量一致性；4）自适应精度重建匹配或超越验证调参的固定先验，且对先验强度误设具有显著更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 完整的ELECTRIC闭环流水线被验证为有效，展示了证据学习与物理引导迭代重建结合的巨大潜力。未来主要研究方向包括形式化的不确定性校准以及网络与重建过程的联合训练。

Abstract: Here we introduce ELECTRIC (Evidential Learning-Enhanced CT Reconstruction via Iterative Correction), a physics-guided Bayesian formulation. An evidential neural network provides an image proposal and an error-predictive epistemic-uncertainty surrogate. The latter is converted into an adaptive precision field and inserted into a Poisson-weighted MAP update. The resulting image-evidence-precision-reconstruction loop treats prior confidence as a learned state variable of iterative reconstruction. In addition to the formulation and theoretical analysis, we report two simulation studies on image slices from the AAPM Mayo Clinic Low-Dose CT dataset: a mechanism-validation pilot using transparent surrogate estimators, and a feasibility study in which a trained Normal-Inverse-Gamma evidential network drives the full closed loop. On held-out patients, the learned prior mean reduces reconstruction error by roughly 70 percent relative to filtered back-projection, the learned epistemic uncertainty is error-predictive and supports selective trust, and the physics-guided update restores measurement consistency while the adaptive-precision reconstruction matches or exceeds a validation-tuned fixed prior and remains markedly more robust to prior-strength misspecification. Together these results demonstrate the complete ELECTRIC closed-loop pipeline, while identifying formal uncertainty calibration and joint training as the principal directions for future work.

</details>


### [2] [Noise-Robust Conditional Flow Matching: Generating Clean Samples from Noisy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00064)
*Adrian Urbański,Gabriel della Maggiora,Artur Yakimovich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出噪声鲁棒条件流匹配（NR-CFM），一种从每张仅有一幅噪声观测中学习干净数据分布的无条件生成模型。对于加性高斯白噪声提供闭式干净端点修正，对更复杂协方差的广义高斯污染采用数据驱动的修正，在科学成像（如低信噪比粒子图像）中取得优于NR-GAN、与Ambient Diffusion相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的输出质量取决于训练数据的质量，而科学成像中获取干净参考数据往往成本高昂、不切实际甚至不可行。直接在噪声测量数据上训练生成模型会重现被污染的数据，因此需要一种能够直接从噪声数据中学习干净种群分布的框架。

Method: 基于条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching, CFM）框架，提出NR-CFM。对于加性白高斯噪声，推导出闭式的干净端点（clean endpoint）修正公式；对于具有更复杂协方差结构的广义高斯污染，则学习一个数据驱动的修正项。模型以每张图像单次噪声观测为输入，学习无条件生成干净分布。

Result: 在评估的各噪声设置下，NR-CFM在大多数情况优于NR-GAN，并在高噪声区间与Ambient Diffusion表现相当。在信噪比低至0.001的科学数据上，NR-CFM能从严重污染的测量中生成合理的粒子图像。

Conclusion: NR-CFM验证了条件流匹配作为从单次噪声观测学习干净分布的天然框架的可行性，在科学成像场景（尤其是极端低信噪比）展现了生成干净高质图像的潜力。

Abstract: Generative models learn the statistical properties of their training data, so high-quality generation depends on clean and representative datasets. In scientific imaging, acquisition often yields noisy measurements, while collecting clean references can be costly, impractical or even unattainable. Training directly on these measurements results in a model that reproduces the corrupted data. This can be circumvented by learning the clean population distribution directly from the noisy data. Conditional flow matching (CFM) combines a simple regression objective with stable training, efficient sampling, and strong image-generation performance, making it a natural framework for this setting. We introduce Noise-Robust Conditional Flow Matching (NR-CFM), an unconditional generator that learns from one corrupted observation per image. NR-CFM provides a closed-form clean endpoint correction for additive white Gaussian noise and learns a data-driven correction for general Gaussian corruptions with more complex covariance structure. Across the evaluated corruption settings, NR-CFM outperforms NR-GAN in most cases and remains competitive with Ambient Diffusion in the high-noise regime. We further evaluate NR-CFM on scientific data at signal-to-noise ratios as low as $0.001$, where it generates plausible particle images from severely corrupted measurements.

</details>


### [3] [PhysAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Reliable Remote Heart Rate Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00066)
*Yehui Yang,Bo Zhao,Junzhe Cao,Hui Ma,Yue Sun,Wenjin Wang,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysAgent是一个推理时的多智能体候选验证框架，用于远程光电容积描记（rPPG）心率估计。它不训练新模型，也不让多模态大语言模型（MLLM）直接输出心率，而是将多个基础估计器的输出作为生理假设进行验证，使用轻量级Qwen3-VL-4B驱动多智能体推理，结合确定性生理验证器和可复现的数值融合过程，最终输出可靠的心率估计，在多个公开基准上提升了融合的稳定性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rPPG的非接触心率估计中，弱生理信号易受运动、光照变化、遮挡、肤色差异和设备噪声干扰。现有方法通常依赖单一模型直接预测心率或恢复脉搏波形，但不同强估计器对同一视频可能产生相互矛盾但各自看似合理的候选结果，需要一种机制来解决这些冲突。

Method: PhysAgent采用推理时的多智能体候选验证框架：多个基础估计器输出被视为待验证的生理假设；使用轻量级4B MLLM（Qwen3-VL-4B）驱动多智能体对视频条件、信号可靠性和候选不一致性进行推理；通过确定性生理验证器检查融合提案；最终通过可复现的数值融合过程产生心率输出。该方法既不训练新的基础rPPG模型，也不让MLLM直接输出心率。

Result: 在多个公开rPPG基准上的实验表明，PhysAgent在不同数据集和源域设置下均提升了融合的稳定性和可靠性，同时避免了直接MLLM预测或无约束集成融合的不可复现性和生理不一致性问题。

Conclusion: PhysAgent通过将MLLM的推理能力与确定性生理验证相结合，提供了一种更可靠、可复现的rPPG心率估计融合方案，解决了多估计器输出冲突的难题，代码即将开源。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart-rate estimation from facial videos, but its weak physiological signal is easily corrupted by motion, illumination changes, occlusion, skin-appearance variation, and device noise. Existing rPPG methods typically rely on a single model to directly predict heart rate or recover pulse waveforms, while different strong estimators may produce conflicting yet individually plausible candidates for the same video. To resolve these conflicts, we propose PhysAgent, an inference-time multi-agent candidate-verification framework. Unlike direct prediction approaches, PhysAgent neither trains a new base rPPG model nor asks Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to output heart rate directly. In contrast, it treats outputs from multiple base estimators as physiological hypotheses to be verified and uses a lightweight 4B MLLM, Qwen3-VL-4B, to drive multi-agent reasoning over video conditions, signal reliability, and candidate disagreement. A deterministic physiological verifier checks the fusion proposal, and a reproducible numerical fusion process produces the final heart rate. Experimental results on multiple public rPPG benchmarks show that PhysAgent improves fusion stability and reliability across different datasets and source-domain settings, while avoiding the irreproducibility and physiological inconsistency of direct MLLM prediction or unconstrained ensemble fusion. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [4] [Scene2Sound: Auditory-Grounded Soundscape Generation for 3D Gaussian Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00463)
*Masaki Yoshida,Ren Togo,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scene2Sound是一个无需训练的框架，用于为3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）世界生成空间一致的声音景观。它通过听觉接地（auditory grounding）识别场景中的发声物体并将其锚定到持久的三维位置，结合视觉语言模型的多视角检测、高斯集合匹配进行3D实例关联，最终使用标准基于对象的音频引擎实现任意听众姿态下的实时空间化音频。实验表明该方法在保持音频质量的同时，在空间一致性上优于逐视角和单全景基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS技术能创建逼真的3D世界模拟，但这些世界是"无声"的。已有的音频生成方法通常以单一图像或视角为条件，导致生成的声音被绑定到该观察层面，当听众在场景中移动时声音无法保持一致。因此需要一种能在3D世界中生成随听众位置变化而保持空间一致的声音景观的方法。

Method: Scene2Sound框架包含三个关键步骤：（1）从输入世界中选择能联合覆盖场景的视角；（2）使用视觉语言模型识别发声物体；（3）通过高斯集合匹配将多视角检测关联为3D实例。每个声源随后获得生成的音频，并由标准基于对象的音频引擎在任意听众姿态下进行实时空间化。此外还提出了两个空间一致性度量指标：一个测试渲染音频是否对听众运动做出一致响应，另一个测试声源是否得到放置时未使用的视角的支持。

Result: 在生成的3DGS世界以及从真实360度拍摄生成的3DGS场景上，Scene2Sound保持了与强逐视角基线相当的音频质量，同时在空间一致性方面优于逐视角和单全景管线。用户研究确认了其感知上的优势。

Conclusion: Scene2Sound通过训练无关的框架成功实现了3DGS世界中的空间一致音频生成，验证了听觉接地和高斯集合匹配方法在实现3D声景生成中的有效性，为沉浸式3D世界体验提供了重要补充。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) turns captured or generated imagery into photorealistic 3D world simulations that users can freely explore, yet these worlds remain silent. Because existing audio generation methods condition on a single image or viewpoint, their sound is tied to that observation and cannot stay consistent while a listener moves. We introduce the task of generating a spatially consistent soundscape for a given 3DGS world through auditory grounding, identifying which objects in the world should emit sound and anchoring each to a persistent 3D position, and present Scene2Sound, a training-free framework built on this grounding. From the input world alone, our pipeline selects viewpoints that jointly cover the scene, identifies sound-emitting objects with a vision-language model, and associates the multi-view detections into 3D instances through Gaussian set matching, which measures the overlap between the Gaussian sets that render each detection. Each source then receives generated audio that a standard object-based audio engine spatializes in real time at arbitrary listener poses. We further propose two spatial-consistency metrics, one testing whether rendered audio responds consistently to listener motion and one testing whether the claimed sources are supported by views held out from their placement. On a curated set of generated 3DGS worlds and on 3DGS scenes generated from real-world 360-degree captures, Scene2Sound preserves the audio quality of strong per-viewpoint baselines while remaining spatially consistent where per-viewpoint and single-panorama pipelines do not, and a user study confirms the perceptual benefit. Project page: https://masaki-lmd.github.io/scene2sound/.

</details>


### [5] [SafeBuild-Bench: A Temporal-Robust Construction Safety Benchmark with Graph-Enhanced Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00068)
*Yi Cui,Zilin Wang,Yijie Xu,Qianyi Cai,Huizai Yao,Shuai Jiang,Bingzhuo Zhong,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SafeBuild-Bench，一个基于元数据驱动的建筑安全基准测试，用于评估多模态大语言模型在真实时变和场地变化条件下的建筑安全理解能力。该基准从 10 万+工业图像-文本记录中挖掘，包含 3,314 个任务实例和 3,000+专家验证图像。同时提出 GEMS（图增强多模态选择流水线），结合代理模型混淆信号与图多样性，从冗余流中识别信息丰富的候选样本，在小型数据预算下保持鲁棒性能。实验表明现有 MLLM 在 SafeBuild-Bench 上表现不佳，最佳总分接近 60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑安全模型主要针对受控图像中的常见物体识别，无法处理真实部署中的具体风险（如工人站在无护栏的脚手架边缘）。而真实巡检档案存在冗余、长尾分布，且跨场地和月份不断变化，亟需一个能在真实时空变化条件下评估多模态大语言模型建筑安全能力的基准。

Method: 构建 SafeBuild-Bench：从 100K+工业图像-文本记录中挖掘，包含多项选择危害识别和自由形式危害描述两类任务，共 3,314 个任务实例。开发 GEMS 流水线：使用代理模型混淆信号结合基于图的多样性选择策略，从冗余数据流中筛选高质量候选，用于可扩展的专家验证。

Result: GEMS 在公开指令微调数据上，以小数据预算保留了鲁棒性导向的性能。在 SafeBuild-Bench 上，当前主流多模态大语言模型的建筑安全理解能力仍远不可靠，最佳综合得分接近 60。

Conclusion: 现有 MLLM 在真实建筑安全场景下的理解能力仍有显著不足，SafeBuild-Bench 为该领域提供了首个元数据驱动的现实基准，GEMS 为高质量数据筛选提供了可扩展方案。论文开源了基准、评估脚本和 GEMS 代码库。

Abstract: Construction-safety models must handle concrete deployment risks, such as a worker standing near a scaffold edge without guardrails, rather than only recognize common objects in curated images. Yet real inspection archives are redundant, long-tailed, and collected across changing sites and months. We introduce SafeBuild-Bench, a metadata-driven benchmark for evaluating multimodal large language models on construction safety under realistic temporal and site variation. It is mined from 100K+ industrial image-text records and contains 3,314 task instances from over 3,000 expert-verified images, covering multiple-choice hazard identification and free-form hazard description. To make expert verification scalable, we develop GEMS, a graph-enhanced multimodal selection pipeline that combines a proxy-model confusion signal with graph-based diversity to identify informative candidates from redundant streams. On public instruction-tuning data, GEMS-selected subsets preserve robustness-oriented performance under small data budgets. On SafeBuild-Bench, current MLLMs remain far from reliable construction-safety understanding, with the best overall score near 60. We release the benchmark, evaluation scripts, and GEMS codebase at https://github.com/safebuild/gems.

</details>


### [6] [CultureVidBench: Benchmarking Cultural Understanding in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01942)
*Xianjing Han,Yuhan Su,Yang Deng,Dong Ma,Wee Peng Tay,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CultureVidBench，一个用于评估文本到视频（T2V）生成模型文化理解能力的综合基准，包含1,000个精心设计的提示词，覆盖12个国家、6大洲、8个文化区域和14个文化维度。通过人工用户研究和MLLM自动评估七种代表性T2V模型，发现当前模型在语义遵循和视觉质量上表现良好，但在捕捉细粒度文化细节方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V生成模型进展迅速，但其文化多样性表征能力尚未被充分探索。现有基准主要评估感知质量、物理合理性和文本-视频对齐，未能直接评估视频是否捕捉到特定文化的物品、动作、仪式、可见文字或音频线索。因此需要专门的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建CultureVidBench基准，包含1,000个策划提示词，覆盖12个国家、6大洲、8个文化区域和14个文化维度，分为物质文化、社会实践与表演、仪式与典礼三大类。该基准强调动态和多模态文化表征（如社会互动、仪式流程、文化适配的可见文字与音频）。评估采用人工用户研究和基于MLLM的自动评估两种方式，从文化保真度、多模态文化渲染、语义遵循和感知质量四个维度对七种代表性T2V模型进行评测。

Result: 当前T2V模型在语义遵循和视觉质量方面表现强劲，但在忠实捕捉细粒度文化细节方面经常失败，特别是在代表性不足的地区、仪式及多模态文化线索（如可见文字和音频）方面表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 研究表明现有T2V生成模型在文化理解和表征方面仍存在显著短板，尤其是对细粒度文化细节、非主流文化区域及多模态文化线索的捕捉能力不足，凸显了该领域需要进一步改进的方向。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation models have advanced rapidly, yet their ability to represent diverse cultural contexts remains underexplored. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on perceptual quality, physical plausibility, and text-video alignment, but do not directly assess whether generated videos capture culturally specific objects, actions, rituals, visible text, or audio cues. We introduce CultureVidBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating cultural understanding in T2V generation. CultureVidBench contains 1,000 curated prompts covering 12 countries, 6 continents, 8 cultural regions, and 14 cultural aspects organized into three categories: material culture, social practice & performance, and ritual & ceremony. Designed specifically for video generation, CultureVidBench emphasizes dynamic and multimodal cultural representation, including social interactions, ritual procedure, and culturally appropriate visible text and audio. We evaluate seven representative T2V models through human user studies and MLLM-based automatic assessment across cultural faithfulness, multimodal cultural rendering, semantic adherence, and perceptual quality. Results show that although current models achieve strong semantic adherence and visual quality, they often fail to faithfully capture fine-grained cultural details, particularly for underrepresented regions, rituals, and multimodal cultural cues.

</details>


### [7] [Empirical investigation of 3D CT Foundation Models and Unsupervised Adaptation for Head and Neck Cancer Recurrence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00071)
*Bilel Guetarni,Feryal Windal,David Pasquier,Halim Benhabiles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多个3D CT基础模型在头颈癌无复发生存预测中的跨数据集泛化能力，基于两个公共数据集共3,644名患者，测试了多种适应策略和模态融合方法。结果显示外部验证时性能显著下降，跨影像分布泛化仍具挑战；将影像特征与临床数据融合仍是最准确的预后预测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT基础模型作为传统影像组学的替代方案日益普及，但对其学习表示能否跨多样化临床环境泛化、以及是否需要针对下游任务进行适应（adaptation）以释放其全部潜力，目前缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 在头颈癌无复发生存预测任务上，对多个3D CT基础模型进行基准测试；数据集涉及两个公共数据集共3,644名患者；评估了不同的适应策略（adaptation strategies）和模态融合机制（modality fusion mechanisms）。

Result: 研究发现难以识别能跨不同影像分布稳定泛化的特征，外部验证队列上出现显著性能下降；影像特征与临床数据融合是最准确的预后预测方法。

Conclusion: 当前一代3D CT基础模型在跨临床场景实现通用泛化方面仍面临重大挑战，但影像与临床多模态融合策略依然是最有效的预后预测路径。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of 3D CT foundation models has opened new avenues for predictive modeling from CT imaging, offering a compelling alternative to traditional radiomics which is known to suffer from reproducibility issues and sensitivity to acquisition protocol variations. Yet, as these models grow in availability, a critical need arises to evaluate how well their learned representations generalize across diverse clinical settings and whether adaptation to specific downstream tasks is necessary to unlock their full potential. To address these questions, we benchmarked several 3D CT foundation models for predicting recurrence-free survival in head and neck cancer across two public datasets totaling 3,644 patients, evaluating various adaptation strategies and modality fusion mechanisms. Our findings reveal persistent difficulty in identifying features that generalize consistently across different imaging distributions, as evidenced by significant performance drops on external validation cohorts. Ultimately, the integration of imaging features with clinical data remains the most accurate approach for prognostic prediction, though achieving universal generalization across varied clinical contexts continues to represent a substantial challenge for the current generation of models.

</details>


### [8] [Déjà Cue: Localizing States in Object Histories via Vocabulary-Relative Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02044)
*Haofan Cao,Zhichao You,Yunkai Yang,Liang Guo,Jie Wang,Chongshou Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Déjà Cue，一个无需训练的框架，通过将可替代的状态描述转化为词汇相对坐标系，对冻结编码器的帧级特征进行校准，从而实现身份条件下（identity-conditioned）的状态区间检索（state-moment retrieval）。在78个VOST历史片段上，仅改变查询参照（保持时间扫描固定），R@1 (tIoU 0.5) 从10.3%提升至20.5%，Top-1 tIoU从16.0%提升至21.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频对象跟踪可以追踪同一物体跨视觉变化的过程，但无法直接判断对象何时是空的/满的、完整的/被切割的状态。绝对图像-文本相似度评分将每个描述独立评价，但由于所有可见帧都描绘同一目标，共享的对象兼容性会掩盖区分状态所需的关键证据，导致状态区间检索困难。因此需要利用备选状态描述作为参照基准来定位目标状态区间。

Method: Déjà Cue框架的工作流程如下：(1) 将可替代的状态描述构建为词汇相对坐标系；(2) 从每个描述中减去状态平衡质心（state-balanced centroid）进行校准；(3) 使用冻结编码器（frozen encoder）对连续可见片段内多个持续时间进行扫描。整个框架无需训练，仅改变查询参照即可提升检索性能。

Result: 在78个VOST历史片段上，保持时间扫描固定、仅改变查询参照，R@1 (tIoU 0.5) 从10.3%翻倍至20.5%，Top-1 tIoU从16.0%提升至21.5%。候选排名分析表明，词汇相对查询在同一候选集合内将有用的区间排名推得更高。

Conclusion: 相关的状态描述可以作为对象特定的、查询时刻的坐标系来读取冻结视觉表示，提供了一种无需训练且有效的状态区间检索方法。备选状态之间的相对比较（而非绝对独立打分）是识别的关键。

Abstract: Tracking links observations of the same object through visual change, yet cannot by itself determine when the object is empty or filled, intact or cut. We formulate identity-conditioned state-moment retrieval: given a tracked-object history and alternative state descriptions, localize an interval in which each described state holds. Absolute image-text similarity scores descriptions independently; because every visible frame depicts the same target, shared object compatibility can obscure the state evidence needed to identify the target interval. The alternatives provide the missing reference: evidence for one state should be measured against the others. We introduce Déjà Cue, a training-free framework that turns these alternatives into a vocabulary-relative coordinate system. It subtracts their state-balanced centroid from each description, calibrates frame scores, and scans multiple durations within contiguous visible runs using a frozen encoder. On 78 VOST histories, holding the temporal scan fixed and changing only the query reference nearly doubles R@1 at tIoU 0.5 from 10.3\% to 20.5\% and raises Top-1 tIoU from 16.0\% to 21.5\%. Candidate-rank analyses show that vocabulary-relative queries rank useful intervals higher within the same candidate set. Related state descriptions can therefore serve as an object-specific, query-time coordinate system for reading frozen visual representations.

</details>


### [9] [Volcanic Clouds Detection through QCNN and Geostationary Satellite Multispectral Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00072)
*Federica Torrisi,Claudia Corradino,Alessandro Grilli,Tommaso Catuogno,Mattia Verducci,Elisabetta Paladino,Luigi Giannelli,Alessandro Sebastianelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索了混合量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）在卫星图像火山云分类中的应用，评估了2量子比特和4量子比特两种QCNN变体在SEVIRI图像数据集上的表现，并与纯经典架构进行了性能对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火山云检测对航空安全和气候评估至关重要，但由于火山云与气象云相似性高、喷发信号多变以及地球静止卫星光谱采样粗糙等原因，准确检测仍具挑战。量子计算和量子机器学习为地球观测数据分析提供了新的可能性，有望解决传统方法在复杂光谱/空间特征提取上的局限。

Method: 将量子计算层集成到经典卷积框架中，构建混合量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）。设计了2量子比特和4量子比特两种QCNN变体，用于对SEVIRI卫星图像进行分类，数据集包含含火山云（灰烬、SO2或混合成分）场景及非火山背景场景。最终与纯经典架构进行性能比较。

Result: 论文报告了两种QCNN变体在SEVIRI火山云图像分类任务上的表现，并将其与纯经典架构进行了对比，具体数值结果需查阅全文。

Conclusion: 该工作初步验证了混合QCNN在卫星遥感火山云分类中的潜力，为量子增强模型在地球观测领域的应用提供了探索性证据。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing are opening new possibilities for Earth Observation (EO) data analysis. Quantum machine learning (QML) approaches offer novel ways to process information by exploiting quantum phenomena such as superposition and entanglement. These capabilities have motivated the exploration of whether quantum-enhanced models can address long-standing challenges in satellite remote sensing, where complex spectral and spatial signals often require sophisticated feature extraction. Among various fields of application, EO data allow the global monitoring of volcanic clouds and are crucial for aviation safety, hazard assessment, real-time eruption response, and evaluation of volcanic impacts on climate. Yet accurate detection of volcanic clouds remains difficult due to their similarity with meteorological clouds, the variability of eruption signatures, and the coarse spectral sampling of geostationary sensors. In this work, the potential of hybrid quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) for the classification of satellite images containing volcanic clouds was investigated. These architectures integrate quantum computational layers into a classical convolutional framework. Two QCNN variants (with 2 and 4 qubits) have been considered to evaluate their ability to classify a dataset of SEVIRI images, including scenes with volcanic clouds (composed of ash, $SO_2$, or mixed components) as well as non-volcanic backgrounds. Finally, the performance of the hybrid QCNN models was compared with that of purely classical architectures.

</details>


### [10] [MIEScore: Human-Aligned Evaluation for Multi-Source Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02059)
*Zitong Xu,Huiyu Duan,Xinyun Zhang,Weifei Xiong,Tianyi Zheng,Xiongkuo Min,Qiang Hu,Zhengxue Cheng,Bo Li,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIE-Bench，这是首个大规模多源图像编辑（MIE）基准，包含3,000个编辑实例、16个任务、36K张编辑图像和108K+个平均意见分数（MOSs），并基于此提出了MIEScore——一个通过技能优化和多维监督微调增强的多模态大语言模型评估器，用于提供人类对齐的MIE反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑评估方法主要聚焦于单图编辑任务，忽略了更具挑战性的多源图像编辑（MIE）场景。尽管Nano-Banana-Pro和GPT-Image-2等统一多模态模型已展现出MIE能力，但缺乏全面且与人类对齐的基准来衡量这些能力，因此亟需构建MIE专用的大规模基准。

Method: 1) 构建MIE-Bench：包含3,000个编辑实例，覆盖16个任务（如物体合成、人物-背景合成、跨图像风格融合），每个实例涉及两张以上源图像和一个编辑提示；由12个SOTA编辑模型生成36K张编辑图像，并收集108K+个MOS分数，涵盖视觉质量、指令遵循和属性保持三个维度。2) 提出MIEScore：基于MLLM的评估模型，采用技能优化（skill optimization）和多维监督微调（multi-dimensional supervised fine-tuning）来增强与人类偏好的一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，MIEScore在与人类偏好对齐方面达到SOTA性能，并能很好地泛化到其他IEQA数据集。

Conclusion: MIE-Bench填补了MIE领域缺乏基准的空白，MIEScore提供了一种人类对齐的多维图像编辑评估方案，数据集和模型均已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in unified multimodal models have significantly improved text-guided image editing abilities. In particular, models such as Nano-Banana-Pro and GPT-Image-2 demonstrate emerging capabilities in multi-source image editing (MIE), including tasks such as object synthesis, person-background composition, and cross-image style fusion. However, existing benchmarks and image editing assessment (IEQA) methods remain primarily focused on single-image editing tasks and largely overlook the more challenging setting of MIE. This highlights the urgent need for a comprehensive and human-aligned benchmark for MIE. To this end, we introduce MIE-Bench, the first large-scale multiple image editing benchmark with fine-grained human preference annotations. Specifically, MIE-Bench includes 3,000 editing instances across 16 tasks, each involving more than two source images and an editing prompt, together with 36K edited images produced by 12 state-of-the-art editing models and over 108K mean opinion scores (MOSs) covering visual quality, instruction following, and attribute preservation. Based on MIE-Bench, we propose MIEScore, a multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based evaluation model enhanced with skill optimization and multi-dimensional supervised fine-tuning, to provide human-aligned feedback for MIE. Extensive experiments show that MIEScore achieves state-of-the-art performance in aligning with human preferences and generalizes well across other IEQA datasets. Both the dataset and the model are available at https://github.com/IntMeGroup/MIEScore.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Random Partitioning: Unsupervised Spatio-Temporal Stratification for Cohort Balancing in Longitudinal Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00073)
*Qinghui Liu,Jon André Ottesen,Atle Bjørnerud,Kyrre Eeg Emblem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于纵向医学影像数据集划分的三方数据集分析框架（Tripartite Dataset Analytics Framework），通过表征空间网格完整性、多参数强度指纹和纵向时间轨迹，并结合肘部优化的K-means聚类与簇内比例分层抽样，实现了无监督的时空队列平衡标准操作程序（SOP）。在149例纵向增强T1加权脑MRI队列上，该方法将最大跨子集强度偏差从随机划分的34.1%降至2.1%以下，显著提高了数据集划分的可审计性和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纵向医学影像的深度学习研究中，数据集划分是一个基础但常被忽视的环节。对小型临床队列进行朴素随机打乱会导致训练集、验证集和测试集之间出现协变量偏移和时间采样不平衡，使下游模型面临分布外评估的风险。这种脆弱性迫切需要一种可审计、系统化的数据集划分方法来保障模型评估的可靠性。

Method: 该方法包含两部分：(1) 三方数据集分析框架，系统地表征空间网格完整性、多参数强度指纹以及纵向时间轨迹，量化临床队列典型的重尾特征分布和不规则、间歇性采样间隔；(2) 无监督时空队列平衡SOP，采用肘部优化的K-means聚类，在标准化六维联合强度-时间特征空间上进行聚类，并结合簇内比例分层抽样实现队列划分。通过蒙特卡洛压力测试（10个随机种子、3种划分配置）验证方法的稳定性。

Result: 在149例纵向增强T1加权脑MRI队列上，该协议将最大跨子集强度偏差从随机划分下的34.1%降低至2.1%以下，同时使纵向随访间隔紧密对齐总体均值。蒙特卡洛压力测试表明该方法的对齐效果在不同随机种子和划分配置下保持紧密有界，与随机划分的显著变异性形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该协议提供了一种可复现、可泛化的数据集工程程序，适用于长度可变的纵向临床影像工作流，有效缓解了传统随机划分带来的协变量偏移和时间采样不平衡问题，提高了深度学习模型评估的可靠性和可审计性。

Abstract: Rigorous dataset partitioning is a foundational, yet frequently overlooked, prerequisite for reliable deep learning in longitudinal medical imaging. Naively shuffling small clinical cohorts routinely introduces covariate shifts and temporal sampling imbalances across training, validation, and test subsets, exposing downstream models to out-of-distribution evaluation. We address this vulnerability with an auditable Tripartite Dataset Analytics Framework that systematically characterizes spatial grid integrity, multi-parametric intensity fingerprints, and longitudinal temporal trajectories, quantifying the heavy-tailed feature dispersion and irregular, episodic sampling intervals typical of real-world clinical cohorts. Building on this characterization, we formalize an unsupervised spatio-temporal cohort-balancing standard operating procedure (SOP) that combines elbow-optimized K-means clustering over a standardized, six-dimensional joint intensity-temporal feature space with intra-cluster proportionate stratified sampling. On a longitudinal, contrast-enhanced $T1$-weighted brain MRI cohort (N=149), the protocol reduces the maximum cross-subset intensity bias from 34.1% under conventional random shuffling to under 2.1%, while aligning longitudinal follow-up intervals closely around the population mean. Monte Carlo stress testing across ten random seeds and three split configurations confirms that this alignment remains tightly bounded, in clear contrast to the substantial variability of random partitioning. The resulting protocol offers a reproducible, generalizable procedure for cohort engineering in variable-length longitudinal clinical imaging workflows.

</details>


### [12] [Deep Multimodal Fusion Detection through Spatial Mask and Channel Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02092)
*Guandi Wang,Ming Li,Yunsen Xing,Junle Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Attention-Driven Complementarity Resampling (ADCR)的框架，用于跨模态目标检测。该方法通过语义掩码交换和可学习的通道竞争机制，解决双骨干网络中单模态统计特性过拟合/过度专业化的问题，在多个数据集上取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征级融合方法主要是在两个模态之间进行加权并在统一表示空间中融合，这容易导致在双骨干架构中对单一模态的统计特性产生过拟合或过度专业化，从而限制了跨模态目标检测的泛化能力。

Method: 基于共享通道-空间注意力机制，提出两个核心模块：(1) 语义掩码交换：在训练阶段主动混合模态边界，迫使骨干网络学习不依赖固定模态标签的广义特征；(2) 可学习通道竞争：以通道级、可学习的方式采样和聚合特征。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法有效，并在现有最先进方法中取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 提出的ADCR框架通过语义掩码交换和可学习通道竞争机制，有效缓解了双模态融合中的过拟合问题，提升了跨模态目标检测的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep multimodal fusion for object detection has demonstrated good performance through mining modal characteristics. However, existing feature-level fusion methods mainly weigh between two modalities and unify them in a unified representation space. This can lead to overfitting or over-specialization of the statistical properties of a single modality within a dual-backbone architecture. This paper proposes an Attention-Driven Complementarity Resampling framework for robust improvement of cross-modality object detection. Based on a shared channel spatial attention mechanism, we first introduce the semantic mask exchange to actively mix the boundaries of the modalities during the training phase, forcing the backbone network to learn generalized features without relying on fixed modal labels. Then we propose a learnable channel competition to sample and aggregate features in a channel-wise and learnable way. Our experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed method is effective and yields competitive results among existing state-of-the-art approaches. The source code is provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [13] [Explainable Multimodal AI for Adaptive Calibration of Archaeological Sensing Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00074)
*Nevio Dubbini,Daniel P. van Helden,Claudia Sciuto,Martina Naso,Arthur Leck,Clement Joubert,Heeli C. Schechter,Remy Chapoulie,Gabriele Gattiglia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态机器学习框架，用于考古数字化工作流程中的标定监测、质量评估和自适应采集支持。该框架通过统一流水线（结合确定性质量指标、统计特征表示、机器学习分类、异常检测和可解释人工智能）处理摄影测量3D重建、高光谱成像、X射线荧光光谱和拉曼光谱，在不替代仪器级标定的前提下增加算法层，评估采集数据是否统计一致、物理合理并适合下游多模态集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考古数字化涉及多种传感模态（摄影测量、高光谱、XRF、拉曼），各模态的采集质量波动难以统一监控。现有方法主要依赖仪器级标定，缺乏系统性的采集质量评估层，无法跨模态识别采集退化问题。作者希望构建一个通用的算法层，能够自动判别采集数据质量并解释退化原因，同时支持自适应采集策略。

Method: 框架采用多层结构化流水线：首先为每种传感模态构造包含几何、光谱、空间和统计属性的结构化特征空间；然后结合监督和无监督学习方法（机器学习分类与异常检测）区分可接受与有问题的采集；引入XAI技术解释退化的潜在原因；最后通过特征空间偏差与采集级纠正措施之间的映射，支持自适应反馈和资源感知的采集策略。

Result: 在多种考古多模态数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法能够捕捉有意义的采集变异性，并在异构传感模态之间实现稳健的质量评估。

Conclusion: 该框架为考古数字化提供了一种通用的、可解释的采集质量监控方案，能够跨异构模态统一评估采集数据，并为自适应采集提供决策支持，未来有望推广至更多数字化应用场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a multimodal machine-learning framework for calibration monitoring, quality assessment, and adaptive acquisition support in archaeological digitisation workflows. The proposed approach operates across photogrammetric 3D reconstruction, hyperspectral imaging, X-ray fluorescence spectroscopy, and Raman spectroscopy through a unified pipeline combining deterministic quality indicators, statistical feature representations, machine-learning classification, anomaly detection, and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI). Rather than replacing instrument-level calibration, the framework introduces an additional algorithmic layer that evaluates whether acquisitions are statistically consistent, physically plausible, and suitable for downstream multimodal integration. For each sensing modality, acquisitions are represented through structured feature spaces encoding geometric, spectral, spatial, and statistical properties. These representations are used to identify degradation patterns such as reconstruction artefacts, illumination inconsistencies, spectral distortions, detector instability, baseline fluctuations, and low signal-to-noise conditions. Supervised and unsupervised learning methods are combined with XAI techniques to support both automatic discrimination between acceptable and problematic acquisitions and interpretation of the underlying causes of degradation. The framework additionally supports adaptive feedback and resource-aware acquisition strategies by linking feature-space deviations to acquisition-level corrective actions. Experimental results obtained on multimodal archaeological datasets demonstrate that the proposed methodology captures meaningful acquisition variability and enables robust quality assessment across heterogeneous sensing modalities.

</details>


### [14] [K-space Gaussian Representation for Parallel MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00075)
*Yu Guan,Mingyu Hu,Jiale Hu,Zhuoxu Cui,Dong Liang,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出K空间高斯表示（KGR），这是首个直接在原生k空间域构建的显式连续表示方法。KGR利用共享空间几何的Gabor-Gaussian基元参数化连续信号，并通过投影到低秩流形来约束多线圈信号的代数结构，配合频率自适应拟合策略，在多个数据集和采样方案上取得了优于现有重建基线的一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有k空间重建方法通常通过离散采样网格上的插值算子或结构先验来估计缺失样本，虽然能够利用局部插值关系和全局k空间冗余性，但只能重建离散频率系数，无法显式建模底层连续信号，从而限制了重建精度。

Method: KGR将连续k空间信号参数化为具有共享空间几何的Gabor-Gaussian基元，实现紧凑表示并自然保留线圈间相关性；通过将估计表示投影到低秩流形上施加代数约束（源自平滑变化的相位和线圈冗余），并采用频率自适应拟合策略适应不同k空间区域的异质性特征。

Result: 在多个数据集和不同采样方案上的综合验证表明，KGR在定量指标和视觉质量上均一致优于代表性的重建基线方法。

Conclusion: 原生k空间的显式连续参数化为将连续信号建模与结构化低秩重建相结合提供了原则性框架，验证了连续表示在加速MRI重建中的潜力。

Abstract: Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) aims to recover the k-space signal from acquired measurements, where accurate estimation of missing samples is essential for high-fidelity reconstruction. Existing k-space reconstruction methods estimate missing samples through interpolation operators or structure priors defined on discrete sampling grids. Although these formulations effectively exploit local interpolation relationships and global k-space redundancy, they reconstruct only discrete frequency coefficients and therefore do not explicitly model the underlying continuous signal. To overcome this limitation, we propose K-space Gaussian Representation (KGR), the first explicit continuous representation formulated directly in the native k-space domain. Rather than estimating unknown samples on discrete grids, KGR parameterizes the continuous signal using Gabor-Gaussian primitives with shared spatial geometry, yielding a compact representation that naturally preserves inter-coil correlations. Because unconstrained continuous fitting does not necessarily satisfy the intrinsic structural properties of multi-coil signal, the estimated representation is projected onto a low-rank manifold to enforce the algebraic constraints arising from smoothly varying phase and coil redundancy. A frequency-adaptive fitting strategy accommodates the heterogeneous characteristics of different k-space regions. Comprehensive validation across multiple datasets and sampling schemes shows consistent improvements over representative reconstruction baselines in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. These results suggest that explicit continuous parameterization of native k-space provides a principled framework for integrating continuous signal modeling with structured low-rank reconstruction.

</details>


### [15] [Which Modality Decides? Counterfactual Modality Attribution for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00076)
*Vahidin Hasic,Chao Wang,Luis C. Garcia-Peraza-Herrera,David Watson,Senka Krivic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Counterfactual Modality Attribution (CMA)框架，用于量化多模态大语言模型中各模态（图像/文本）对预测的贡献程度，通过反事实生成和Shapley值博弈论公式实现模态归因，在98%的受控案例中正确识别决策主导模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态可解释性方法仅能识别图像显著区域或文本显著token，无法回答"哪个模态在驱动预测"这一根本问题。模型可能产生正确输出但依赖错误的证据来源，掩盖捷径学习和不安全推理。

Method: CMA框架包含两个核心步骤：(1)使用耦合扩散先验生成仅图像、仅文本和联合多模态反事实样本；(2)通过基于Shapley值的合作博弈论公式将反事实转化为原理性的模态归因分数。

Result: 在已知真实模态依赖的受控合成基准上，CMA在98%的案例中正确识别决策驱动模态，并持续优于基线方法。在真实世界的多模态临床数据集上，CMA发现了预测准确率无法暴露的跨模态推理失败。

Conclusion: 模态归因应作为超越特征归因的可解释性补充维度，为安全关键应用中多模态基础模型的审计提供原理性框架。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly support high-stakes decision making by combining complementary information from images and text. While existing explainability methods identify influential image regions or text tokens, they cannot answer a fundamental question: which modality drives a prediction? Consequently, a model may produce the correct output while relying on the wrong source of evidence, masking shortcut learning and unsafe reasoning. We formulate modality attribution as a complementary explainability objective for multimodal foundation models and propose Counterfactual Modality Attribution (CMA), the first framework for quantifying modality-level contributions in MLLMs. CMA generates image-only, text-only, and joint multimodal counterfactuals using coupled diffusion priors and converts them into principled modality attribution scores through a cooperative game-theoretic formulation based on Shapley values. We evaluate CMA on controlled synthetic benchmarks with known ground-truth modality reliance and on a real-world multimodal clinical dataset. CMA correctly identifies the decision-driving modality in 98% of controlled cases and consistently outperforms baselines, revealing failures of cross-modal reasoning that remain invisible to predictive accuracy alone. Our results establish modality attribution as a complementary dimension of explainability beyond feature attribution, providing a principled framework for auditing multimodal foundation models in safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond Accuracy: Auditing Spatial Provenance in Visual Token Pruning for OCR-Critical MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00077)
*Feixiang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Hao Zhang,Xinyue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中视觉token剪枝的传统评估协议。传统方法仅以固定保留预算下的答案质量来评判剪枝效果，忽略了"答案正确但支持其的OCR局部区域token已被全部剪除"的隐性失败模式。作者提出一种证据风险审计方法，将答案行为与token的几何来源溯源、干预措施和实际成本相结合。在图像不重叠的locked验证集上，Qwen Target在30%保留率下准确率(0.786)几乎与Full(0.783)持平，但同预算下不同选择器（Target/Random/Grid）的正向支持覆盖差异显著（0.620/0.270/0.318），揭示准确率无法暴露的模型特异性质量-风险-可追溯性前沿。材料化前缀带来最高4.32倍批量预填充加速和76.4%的增量峰值内存降低；但完整验证表明有利的target验证点并不意味着任务通用的压缩有效。结论是视觉token剪枝应在报告质量与压缩率的同时，报告存留token的空间溯源和实际成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝评估仅以固定预算下的答案质量为依据，忽视了文本密集MLLM中一个关键失败模式：即便OCR区域对应的token全部被剪除，答案仍可能正确。这种"答案正确但证据缺失"的盲区使得现有评估协议无法捕获剪枝对可追溯性和可靠性的真实影响，需要引入证据风险审计来弥补。

Method: 提出证据风险审计框架，将答案行为与三类信号耦合：(1) token的几何来源溯源（确定存留token是否可回溯到支撑答案的OCR区域）；(2) 受控干预测试；(3) 实际计算成本。采用透明、免训练的选择器（Target/Random/Grid）隔离出可控操作点，并在图像不重叠的锁定数据集上进行验证，配套检测器测试和外部方法对比。

Result: Qwen Target在30%保留率下准确率0.786 vs Full的0.783（配对差异+0.003，95% CI [-0.014, +0.020]），但同预算下Target/Random/Grid的正向支持覆盖分差显著：0.620/0.270/0.318。跨Qwen3-VL-8B、LLaVA-1.5-7B和InternVL3.5-8B三个模型均发现准确率无法暴露的模型特定质量-风险-可追溯性前沿。材料化前缀获得最高4.32倍批量预填充加速和76.4%更低的增量峰值内存；但TextVQA/DocVQA完整验证表明target验证点的优势不具备任务泛化性。

Conclusion: 视觉token剪枝的评估不能仅以准确率和压缩率为唯一标准，必须同步报告存留token的空间溯源比例（即证据可追溯性）和实际计算成本。准确率几乎无损并不等于证据无损，质量-风险-可追溯性之间存在模型特定的权衡前沿，需要透明、免训练的选择器与受控审计流程来系统刻画。

Abstract: Visual-token pruning is usually judged by answer quality at a fixed retention budget. For text-rich multimodal large language models (MLLMs), this protocol can miss a distinct failure: an answer remains correct even when no retained token is locally traceable to the small OCR region that supports it. We turn this blind spot into an evidence-risk audit that couples answer behavior with geometric token-origin provenance, interventions, and realized cost; transparent training-free selectors isolate controlled operating points. On locked image-disjoint confirmation, Qwen Target at 30% retention has observed accuracy 0.786 versus 0.783 for Full (paired image-cluster difference +0.003, 95% CI [-0.014, +0.020]), yet same-budget Target, Random, and Grid retain sharply different positive-support coverage: 0.620, 0.270, and 0.318. Across Qwen3-VL-8B, LLaVA-1.5-7B, and InternVL3.5-8B, matched controls, interventions, detector tests, and external methods reveal model-specific quality-risk-traceability frontiers that accuracy alone does not expose. Materialized prefixes yield up to 4.32x batch-prefill speedup and 76.4% lower incremental peak memory; full-validation TextVQA and DocVQA further show that favorable target-verification points do not imply task-general compression. Visual-token pruning should therefore report surviving spatial provenance and realized cost alongside quality and compression.

</details>


### [17] [Device-First Feedback: Toward Mobile-Native LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00078)
*Saif U Din,Muhammad Ahsan Hussain,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从LLM生成CNN架构到真实移动设备部署的全自动闭环流水线，涵盖QLoRA微调、GPU评估、INT8导出和物理设备延迟基准测试。在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，闭环GPU微调并不保证移动端指标单调提升，尤其在更难的分类任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，LLM生成的CNN只在GPU上验证精度，但真实移动设备部署还受INT8 TensorFlow Lite导出、委托选择和端上延迟三者共同制约。现有方法忽略了从GPU精度到物理设备可用性之间的鸿沟，缺乏自动化的端到端部署闭环。

Method: 构建全自动移动部署流水线：从QLoRA微调架构生成LLM开始，经过GPU评估、INT8导出、真实设备循环基准测试，再到基于门控机制增强训练语料库，形成闭环迭代。流水线完全脚本化，支持中断恢复，无需人工干预地逐周期运行。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，cycle 1通过门控并被接受，移动部署分数比基线提升约25.6倍（平均量化精度46.9%）；后续周期提升GPU精度但未通过移动端非递减门控。在CIFAR-100上，基线保留最佳移动端分数，迭代提升了GPU精度最多26.2%但无法在设备上超越cycle 0，训练池在首个被接受周期后停留在19个样本。

Conclusion: 两个基准实验共同表明：闭环GPU微调不保证移动端指标单调提升，尤其对更困难的分类任务。需要多数据集、真实设备端测量来全面检验部署目标。作者发布了每周期指标（含95%置信区间）、全部图表和完整复现命令。

Abstract: Deploying convolutional neural networks generated by large language models (LLMs) on real mobile hardware requires more than GPU validation accuracy: INT8 TensorFlow Lite export, delegate selection, and on-device latency jointly determine whether a model is usable. We present an automated mobile deployment pipeline that closes the loop from QLoRA fine-tuning of an architecture-generating LLM through GPU evaluation, INT8 export, and physical-device benchmarking to gated augmentation of the training corpus. The pipeline is fully scripted and runs cycle-by-cycle without manual intervention, with resume support after interruptions. We evaluate the same frozen protocol on two benchmarks, CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, on a Samsung SM-P613 tablet (seed 42, 20 models per cycle, cycles 0-6). On CIFAR-10, cycle 1 is gate-accepted and improves the mobile deployment score approximately 25.6x over the baseline with a mean quantized accuracy of 46.9%; later cycles raise GPU accuracy but fail the non-decreasing mobile gate. On CIFAR-100, the pre-QLoRA baseline retains the best mobile score; iterative rounds improve GPU accuracy (up to 26.2%) yet cannot surpass cycle 0 on-device, and the training pool stalls at 19 examples after the first accepted round. Together, the two studies show that closed-loop GPU fine-tuning does not guarantee monotonic mobile gains, especially on harder classification tasks, and that multi-dataset, on-device measurement is needed to stress-test deployment objectives. We release per-cycle metrics with 95% confidence intervals, all figures, and complete reproduction commands.

</details>


### [18] [LeapTalk: Breaking the Latency-Quality Trade-off in Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00079)
*Rongxiang Zhang,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeapTalk是一个用于生成长时、实时说话人视频生成的框架，通过单步桥式蒸馏实现一步生成、高达200 FPS的速度，同时保持身份一致性和时间稳定性，克服了多步扩散的延迟瓶颈和自回归方法中的误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时实时的说话人视频生成面临延迟与质量之间的权衡：多步扩散方法虽质量高但效率低，无法支持流式生成；实时自回归方法则存在误差累积和身份漂移问题。两者均难以兼顾效率与稳定性。

Method: 该方法的核心是单步桥式蒸馏方案：(1) 采用基于布朗桥的数据到数据传输框架替代传统的噪声到数据范式，以持久参考锚定，缓解身份漂移并增强长期时间稳定性；(2) 通过SNR对齐的时间变换Φ(τ)实现异构蒸馏，从预训练的扩散教师模型向学生桥模型平滑迁移知识；(3) 提出音频驱动的无分类器引导机制，在极端步数缩减下保持细粒度的唇部同步。

Result: 大量实验表明，LeapTalk仅需1步即可生成高保真且时间一致的视频，速度高达200 FPS，在效率和稳定性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LeapTalk通过创新的单步桥式蒸馏框架，有效解决了说话人视频生成中延迟与质量之间的权衡问题，实现了实时、稳定且可扩展至任意时长的高质量视频生成。

Abstract: Long-form and real-time talking-head generation remains challenging due to a latency-quality trade-off: inefficient multi-step diffusion prohibits streaming generation, whereas real-time autoregressive approaches suffer from error accumulation and identity drift. To address this drawback, we propose LeapTalk, a novel framework that achieves stable and real-time talking-head generation with a single forward step, scaling to arbitrarily long videos. At the heart of our approach lies a single-step bridge distillation scheme. On the one hand, departing from the conventional noise-to-data paradigm, we introduce a data-to-data transport formulation based on a Brownian bridge. Anchored by a persistent reference, this strategy effectively mitigates identity drift and enhances long-term temporal stability. On the other hand, to enable smooth knowledge transfer from a pre-trained diffusion teacher to the student bridge model, we explore a heterogeneous distillation framework with an SNR-aligned time transformation $Φ(τ)$, which bridges the functional discrepancy between the two models. Moreover, we propose an audio-driven classifier-free guidance mechanism to maintain fine-grained lip synchronization under extreme step reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves high-fidelity and temporally consistent video generation with only 1 step at up to 200 FPS, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both efficiency and stability. Project Page: https://zhangrongxiang.github.io/leaptalk-page/

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Edge Maps: Wavelet-Domain Conditioning for Multi-Adapter Map-to-Satellite Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00083)
*Arisha Prasain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于ControlNet的扩散框架，仅使用可独立获取的制图数据（OpenStreetMap栅格地图及其平稳小波变换子带）作为条件，通过两个ControlNet适配器融合空间结构与频域细节，生成卫星影像，并在尼泊尔等低资源区域数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业地图合作伙伴在低资源区域常不可用，导致卫星底图过时，因此需要从独立维护的制图数据合成卫星影像。现有ControlNet方法通常从目标图像本身提取结构信号（如边缘或分割），但这种方法假设影像已存在，恰恰在合成最需要的场景下受限。现有地图条件方法仅添加边缘检测等空间线索而未利用频域结构。

Method: 提出一种ControlNet扩散框架，条件输入仅为可独立获取的制图数据：OpenStreetMap栅格地图及其平稳小波变换（SWT）子带。两个ControlNet适配器分别在冻结的Stable Diffusion骨干之上，独立对地图表示和波尔表示进行训练，再通过MultiControlNet融合，无需重新训练多输入模型即可联合利用空间结构与频域细节。

Result: 在尼泊尔配对的map-satellite新数据集和Pix2Pix map-satellite基准上评估。组合条件在8项指标-数据集比较中赢得6项（两数据集的SSIM和PSNR，以及我们数据集上Alex和VGG后端的LPIPS），在Pix2Pix两个LPIPS后端上追平map-only并超越wavelet-only。Wavelet-only在两种数据集上取得最低FID，体现逐图像保真度与分布真实感之间的权衡，但由于测试集规模较小及FID小样本偏差，作者对此差异持谨慎态度。

Conclusion: 结合空间结构（OSM地图）与频域结构（SWT子带）的地图到卫星扩散合成方法在大多数图像质量指标上优于仅用单一条件的方法，为数据稀缺的低资源区域提供了一种无需依赖目标影像存在的卫星影像合成途径。FID上的差异需要在更大测试集上进一步验证。

Abstract: Commercial mapping partnerships are often unavailable in low-resource regions, leaving satellite basemaps stale and motivating synthesis of satellite imagery from independently maintained cartographic data. Existing ControlNet-based diffusion methods typically condition on structural signals like edges or segmentation extracted from the target image itself, assuming the imagery already exists and limiting their use exactly where synthesis matters most. Map-conditioned alternatives add cues like edge detection but omit frequency-domain structure. We propose a ControlNet-based diffusion framework conditioned only on cartographic sources obtainable independently of the target imagery: OpenStreetMap (OSM) raster maps and their stationary wavelet transform (SWT) subbands, a conditioning signal previously unexplored for map-to-satellite diffusion. Two ControlNet adapters, trained separately on the map and wavelet representations atop a frozen Stable Diffusion backbone, are fused via MultiControlNet, jointly drawing on spatial structure and frequency detail without retraining a multi-input model. We evaluate on a new paired map-satellite dataset curated for Nepal, a data-scarce, topographically diverse region, alongside the Pix2Pix maps-satellite benchmark. Combined conditioning wins six of eight metric-dataset comparisons -- SSIM and PSNR on both datasets, plus LPIPS (both Alex and VGG backbones) on ours and ties map-only on both Pix2Pix LPIPS backbones while still edging past wavelet-only there. Wavelet-only takes the lowest FID on both datasets, matching the tradeoff between per-image fidelity and distributional realism. We treat this gap cautiously given our modest test-set sizes and FID's known small-sample bias.

</details>


### [20] [From Pixels to PCells: A Neurosymbolic Approach to Photonic Component Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00084)
*Aadarsh Agarwal,Kenaish Al Qubaisi,Dirk Englund*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixCell是一个神经符号系统，通过多模态代理将视觉呈现的光子组件转换为基于几何原语DSL的参数化程序。借助确定性可视化验证器（使评估比生成尝试更廉价），多模态代理在八个组件目标上实现了超过0.9的平均IoU（最高0.974），远超多种子采样与迭代修订方法的0.416。系统还实现了跨材料平台（SOI、SiN、TFLN）的重构、全波仿真验证，并利用同一验证器作为训练奖励，通过LoRA和GRPO微调Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型，将验证器引导下的冠军IoU从0.422提升至0.491。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子组件设计通常依赖专家手工绘制或仿真迭代，缺乏从视觉输入到可执行程序化表示的可靠自动化途径。现有方法（如多种子采样和迭代修订）效果有限（IoU仅0.416），且缺乏可控的评估框架来衡量、迁移和优化视觉到参数化的设计流程。

Method: PixCell采用神经符号架构，包含多模态代理、领域特定语言（DSL）的几何原语、确定性可视化验证器。验证器使评估比生成更便宜，支持迭代修订。此外，同一个可执行验证器还被用作训练奖励（与LoRA和GRPO结合），在不依赖监督演示的情况下微调Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型。

Result: 多模态代理通过PixCell接口和验证器在八个组件目标上的平均IoU超过0.9（最高0.974和0.955），并满足源合同。跨平台研究中，干涉仪重构的程序在220-nm SOI、400-nm SiN和400-nm TFLN平台上满足8.0 nm自由光谱范围目标和原始占位约束。论文中的分光器经SOI全波仿真验证产生对称传播和均衡输出。经验证器引导的LoRA/GRPO训练后，模型在训练未见过的八个论文图上的平均冠军IoU从0.422提升至0.491。

Conclusion: PixCell建立了一个受控框架，用于测量、重新定向和改进视觉到参数的“光子组件设计”，证明了前沿多模态代理能够可靠地理解视觉目标并渲染可执行的参数化表示，同时验证器本身可作为有效的训练信号来源。

Abstract: We present PixCell, a neurosymbolic system in which multimodal agents convert a visually presented photonic component into a parametric program over a small domain-specific language (DSL) of geometric primitives. A system enabling deterministic visual verification renders evaluation asymmetrically cheaper than the generation attempt. While models using multi-seed sampling and iterative revision reach a mean best-turn IoU of only 0.416, multimodal agents through PixCell's interface and verifier consistently exceed 0.9 mean IoU, with scores reaching 0.974 and 0.955 across eight component targets while also satisfying source contracts. These results demonstrate that frontier multimodal agents can reliably understand and render executable parametric representations from visual targets. Using these live parameters, cross-stack studies on an interferometer reconstruct primitive programs that satisfy an 8.0 nm free spectral range target and the original footprint constraint on modeled 220-nm SOI, 400-nm SiN, and 400-nm TFLN stacks. PixCell further carries a paper-derived splitter from visual reconstruction through SOI full-wave simulation, producing symmetric propagation and balanced outputs. Finally, the same executable verifier supplies a training reward and dataset used to train a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model with LoRA and GRPO without supervised demonstrations. On eight training-excluded paper figures, its mean champion IoU rises from 0.422 after eight initial attempts to 0.491 after three verifier-guided revision rounds. These results therefore establish a controlled framework for measuring, retargeting, and improving visual-to-parametric photonic component design.

</details>


### [21] [DS@GT ARC at MEDIQA-CORE-Task-1 2026: Trimodal Model Fusion with Task-Specific Gates for Brain Tumor Subtype Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00086)
*Hoang Thanh Thanh Truong,Charles R. Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DS@GT ARC团队在ImageCLEFmed MEDIQA-CORE 2026任务1（脑肿瘤亚型分类）中的工作。该团队融合了MRI嵌入（NeuroVFM）、组织病理学嵌入（Prov-GigaPath）和自由文本放射学报告，采用三种模态融合架构、两种报告编码器（RadBERT和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）以及生物启发的后处理阶段，在完全多模态条件下达到0.801的平均宏F1分数，超过组织者基线（0.796）并在通过代码验证的团队中排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤诊断是时间敏感的过程，患者可能需要等待数周才能获得最终病理报告，因此需要自动化系统从多模态输入中快速分类肿瘤亚型，以加速诊断流程。

Method: 该方法结合三种预提取的模态嵌入：MRI（NeuroVFM）、组织病理学（Prov-GigaPath）和放射学报告的文本嵌入。探索了三种三模态融合架构、两种报告编码器（RadBERT和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct），并加入了基于生物学动机的后处理阶段来优化分类结果。任务包含三个胶质瘤分类子问题：Level-1分子类型、LGG vs HGG，以及WHO级别。

Result: 在完全多模态条件下达到0.801的平均宏F1分数，超过组织者基线0.796，在通过代码验证的团队中排名第二。额外的模态丢失实验表明，该优势高度依赖于组织病理学模态的可用性；当模态缺失时，系统性能落后于基线。

Conclusion: 该多模态融合系统在完整模态可用时表现优越，但其鲁棒性不足，过度依赖组织病理学模态。代码已在GitHub上开源（https://github.com/dsgt-arc/imageclef-mediqacore-2026）。

Abstract: Brain tumor diagnosis is a time-sensitive process in which patients may wait weeks for a finalized pathology report. This problem motivates automated systems that classify tumor subtype from multimodal inputs. This paper details the DS@GT ARC team's work for ImageCLEFmed MEDIQA-CORE 2026 Task~1, Brain Tumor Subtype Classification. The task evaluates three glioma classification problems: Level-1 Molecular Type, LGG vs HGG, and WHO Grade. We combine pre-extracted MRI (NeuroVFM) and histopathology (Prov-GigaPath) embeddings with free-text radiology reports. Our team explored two trimodal fusion architectures, two report encoders (RadBERT and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), and a biologically motivated post-processing stage. We achieve a mean macro-F1 of 0.801 under the Fully Multimodal condition, exceeding the organizers' baseline of 0.796 and ranking second among the teams whose code passed verification. Additional evaluation across modality-dropping conditions shows that this advantage depends heavily on the availability of the histopathology modality, and that our system falls behind the baseline when modalities are missing. Our code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/imageclef-mediqacore-2026.

</details>

*Lisa Koßmann,Ralf Bartho,Christoph Redies,Johan Wagemans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DODA（美学数据集数据库），一个用于浏览和选择美学研究数据集的Web应用，提供数据集的通用信息及预计算的图像量化属性，以解决美学研究中数据集分散、筛选困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美学实证与计算领域快速增长，产生了大量标注了美学的图像数据集，但这些数据集在标注标准等方面差异巨大，且缺乏集中的开源搜索系统，研究者下载全部数据集才能了解其图像质量和内容细节，费时费力。

Method: 构建了DODA Web应用，集中收录美学研究的重要数据集，提供每个数据集的基本信息（规模、分辨率、标注类型、标注者数量等），并对许多数据集预计算了定量图像属性；论文还讨论了使用DODA选择合适数据集的相关标准和复用数据集的好处。

Result: DODA作为直观的Web应用可供研究者浏览所有重要的美学研究数据集，并提供通用信息与预计算的图像量化属性，促进实证美学和计算美学两个领域的协作。

Conclusion: DODA方案有助于研究者高效筛选合适的数据集，推动数据集复用，并促进实证美学与计算美学领域的跨领域合作。

Abstract: With rapid growth in the fields of empirical and computational aesthetics we have seen a vast increase in large image datasets annotated for aesthetics. As the image databases differ widely in many respects (e.g., different standards for annotation), it can be tedious to find the dataset that fits one's research needs best. The absence of a centralized open-science search system causes additional problems. Currently, researchers typically share dataset links in papers or on diverse platforms like OSF, GitHub or Dropbox. Manually searching for details like image quality and content often requires downloading all datasets. Therefore, we present the Database Of Datasets for Aesthetics (DODA), an intuitive Web application in which researchers can browse all important datasets for aesthetics research. DODA provides general information about these datasets (size, resolution, type of annotation, number of annotators, etc.) and for many of them also precomputed quantitative image properties. We discuss relevant criteria for selecting a suitable dataset with DODA and illustrate the benefits of reusing datasets. Our approach facilitates collaboration across the fields of empirical and computational aesthetics. Keywords: empirical aesthetics, computational aesthetics, machine learning, image annotation, quantitative image properties, Open Science

</details>


### [23] [Video Models as Native 4D Renderers: World-Grounded Conditioning from Animated Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00094)
*Junhao Chen,Mingjin Chen,Henghaofan Zhang,Minglin Chen,Liaoyuan Fan,Boran Zhang,Saining Zhang,Mingze Sun,Hao Zhao,Ruqi Huang,Zhihao Li,Yufei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAR是一种参考引导的4D生成渲染方法，它扩展了Wan2.2相机控制能力，将Plücker射线从仅相机接口扩展为联合相机加几何接口。DAR从动画网格投影神经4D G-buffer（追踪、世界位置和法线），通过加宽的控制适配器注入，同时保留预训练的图像到视频先验。在68个案例的DAR-4D基准上，LoRA版本达到PSNR 23.22、SSIM 0.895、LPIPS 0.134，全微调版本达到PSNR 25.36和SSIM 0.917。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练视频扩散模型可以作为渲染器，当场景状态由动画网格、相机轨迹和参考图像指定时。这引发了一个核心表示问题：什么样的图像格式条件能让视频骨干网络同时服从相机运动和场景内部动画？作者旨在找到一种能同时编码相机运动和内部动画的图像条件表示，而不是仅依赖深度或Plücker射线。

Method: DAR提出将动画网格投影为神经4D G-buffer，包含三个关键通道：(1) tracking追踪，标识携带外观的持久表面元素；(2) world position世界位置，给出当前场景坐标状态；(3) normal法线，提供局部形状信息。这些信息通过加宽的control adapter注入模型，同时保留预训练的图像到视频先验。关键设计选择是追踪与世界位置的配对：深度加上校准射线原则上可以恢复3D，但深度是相机依赖的图表，相机运动与物体运动在其中混合。

Result: 在68个案例的DAR-4D基准上，LoRA DAR达到PSNR 23.22、SSIM 0.895、LPIPS 0.134，比现成的Wan2.2-Depth提高了1.54 dB PSNR；全微调版本达到PSNR 25.36和SSIM 0.917。受控消融实验显示，在每个检查点上用深度替换世界位置会导致PSNR降低1.26-1.55 dB，验证了追踪+世界位置对应作为实用4D渲染条件的有效性。

Conclusion: 追踪加世界位置的成对表示是一种实用的4D渲染条件，优于传统深度表示。DAR在保留预训练图像到视频先验的同时，通过联合相机加几何接口实现了对相机运动和场景内部动画的双重控制，相较于现有深度条件的Wan2.2方法在PSNR上有显著提升。

Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models can act as renderers when the desired scene state is already specified by an animated mesh, a camera trajectory, and a reference image. This 4D generative rendering setting raises a representation question: what image-format condition lets a video backbone obey both camera motion and scene-internal animation? We propose DAR, a reference-guided renderer that extends Wan2.2 camera control from Plücker rays alone to a joint camera-plus-geometry interface. DAR projects a neural 4D G-buffer (tracking, world position, and normal) from the animated mesh and injects it through a widened control adapter while preserving the pretrained image-to-video prior. The central design choice is the pair of tracking and world position. Tracking identifies the persistent surface element that should carry appearance; world position gives its current scene-coordinate state; normal supplies local shape. Depth plus calibrated rays can recover 3D in principle, but depth is a camera-dependent chart in which camera and object motion are mixed. On the 68-case DAR-4D benchmark, LoRA DAR reaches PSNR 23.22, SSIM 0.895, and LPIPS 0.134, improving over off-the-shelf Wan2.2-Depth by 1.54 dB PSNR; a full fine-tune reaches PSNR 25.36 and SSIM 0.917. Matched ablations show that replacing world position by depth reduces PSNR by 1.26--1.55 dB at every checkpoint, supporting tracking+world-position correspondence as a practical 4D rendering condition.

</details>


### [24] [Logographic Character Visual Pretraining via Semantic-based Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00096)
*Daqian Shi,Wei Cao,Xiaoyu Zheng,Lida Shi,Xiaolei Diao,Cedric M John*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉语义和上下文语义的多模态学习方法，用于解决象形文字（如汉字）识别中数据分布不平衡的问题。通过新颖的预训练策略从语言模型中提取每个字的上下文语义来增强深度视觉表征，在多个数据集上的实验表明该方法优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的字符视觉研究（如文字识别、字符图像去噪、历史文本补全）在数据集大且均衡时表现最佳，但真实世界的字符数据集（尤其是像中文这样的表意文字语言）往往存在数据分布不平衡的问题——由于字符使用频率差异和新字符的持续产生，导致稀有类别和类别不均衡问题普遍存在，制约了现有方法的性能。

Method: 提出一种新颖的表意文字识别方法，引入利用字符视觉语义和上下文语义的多模态学习途径。设计了一种新颖的预训练策略，通过从相应语言模型中提取每个字符的上下文语义来增强深度视觉表征，特别针对数据分布不平衡和稀有实例的问题进行优化。

Result: 在多个数据集上进行实验评估字符识别方法的性能，并通过多个下游任务进一步验证对比预训练策略的有效性。实验结果表明，提出的方法相较于最先进的现有方法具有优越性。

Conclusion: 多模态学习（融合视觉语义与上下文语义）结合对比预训练策略，能够有效缓解表意文字数据不平衡和稀有实例带来的性能瓶颈，为字符识别领域提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Current deep learning-based character vision studies, e.g., text recognition, character image denoising, and historical text completion, are offering new solutions for learning, managing, and utilizing character resources. However, the performance of these studies peaks only with large and balanced datasets, which is a rarity with real-world character datasets, especially for logographic character languages, e.g., Chinese. The imbalance in data distribution of logographic characters is a common issue due to differences in character usage frequency and new characters being continuously created. In this paper, we propose a novel method for logographic character recognition, which introduces a multi-modal learning approach using visual semantics and contextual semantics of characters. A novel pre-training strategy is designed to enhance deep visual representations, especially for datasets suffering from issues of imbalanced and rare instances, by extracting the contextual semantics of each character from the corresponding language models. We conduct experiments across various datasets to evaluate our character recognition method and further validate the contrastive pre-training strategy by several downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of our method compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [25] [SPARC-Rad: A Multimodal Benchmark Dataset and Evaluation Pipeline for Spatial and Anatomical Reasoning in Radiology Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00100)
*Satvik Tripathi,Mustafa Ege Seker,Kristian Quevada,Ebubechukwu D Enwerem,Pratham Khandelwal,Emine Meltem,Bera Koca,Shahriar Faghani,Jacinta Arnold,Dania Daye,Tessa S. Cook*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPARC-Rad基准，一个包含300个图像-问题对的多模态基准数据集和评估管道，专门用于评估医学视觉-语言模型在放射学中的空间感知和解剖推理能力，涵盖CT、MRI和X光影像的多种解剖类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉-语言模型基准多聚焦于疾病分类、报告生成或广泛的视觉问答，缺乏对放射学所需的空间和解剖推理能力的专门评估，因此需要一个专门的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 从TCIA采集健康对照影像数据，构建300个图像-问题对，由放射科实习生手工设计并标注问题，评估解剖识别、定位、侧别、区域识别、设备识别和结构间空间关系等六类推理能力；配套标准化提示、结构化输出收集、响应归一化、LLM-as-judge评分、人工质量审查、二元正确性评分和按模态/解剖/推理类型的亚组分析。

Result: SPARC-Rad提供了一个可复用的评估框架，能够系统性地衡量VLM在放射学解剖作为空间系统推理上的能力，支持亚组分析和失败模式分析。

Conclusion: SPARC-Rad可作为未来VLM模型开发、失败模式分析和部署前评估的基础性工具，推动医学影像VLM朝着具备真正放射学空间推理能力的方向发展。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being evaluated for medical imaging, but many available benchmarks emphasize disease classification, report generation, or broad visual question answering rather than the spatial and anatomical reasoning required for radiology. We developed the Spatial Perception and Anatomical Reasoning in Clinical Radiology (SPARC-Rad) Benchmark, a manually curated multimodal benchmark dataset and evaluation pipeline for assessing these capabilities in radiology VLMs. SPARC-Rad includes 300 image-question pairs derived from healthy control imaging studies in The Cancer Imaging Archive (TCIA), spanning CT, MRI, and radiography across the abdomen, chest, breast, neuro, and musculoskeletal categories. Radiology trainees manually designed and annotated questions to evaluate anatomical identification, localization, laterality, regional recognition, device identification, and inter-structure spatial relationships. The evaluation pipeline supports standardized prompting, structured output collection, response normalization, LLM-as-judge grading, human quality review, binary correctness scoring, and subgroup analysis by modality, anatomy, and reasoning type. SPARC-Rad provides a reusable framework for evaluating whether VLMs can provide reasoning for radiologic anatomy as a spatial system, supporting future model development, failure-mode analysis, and pre-deployment assessment.

</details>


### [26] [What Carries the Signal in Pathology Foundation-Model Atlases? A Patient-Level Controlled Benchmark in Breast Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00105)
*Chimdi Walter Ndubuisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以"患者为单位"重构了病理基础模型从组织形态学预测分子程序信号的分析，发现信号真实但主要来自嵌入特征而非几何机制：11个冻结骨干模型、4个基因程序、285个TCGA-BRCA患者，全部预处理在折内拟合，GroupKFold按患者划分。UNI2在所有四个程序中预测最强（免疫程序Spearman rho=0.556），置换检验Holm校正后p=0.0044。嵌入特征显著优于组织组成（ER/腔面、增殖和免疫程序），但基底程序上无显著差异。关键发现是：测地线图本质上是欧几里得结构（因为用欧氏最近邻选边，仅对已选边重新加权），拓扑/几何机制贡献几乎为零，Ridge回归比图与度量解码器好+0.097；驱动基因计数指标在此几乎无信息（91.8%的随机六基因面板恢复>=5/6驱动基因）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有病理基础模型声称能从组织形态学编码分子程序，但证据通常是队列范围的基因排名列表，而非对留出患者的预测。本文旨在以患者为证据单位重新分析，并追问信号究竟来自哪个流水线组件（嵌入、几何机制、解码器等）。

Method: 使用11个冻结骨干模型、4个预指定基因程序、285个TCGA-BRCA患者（配对切片与RNA-seq，44个细胞），按患者进行GroupKFold划分，所有预处理在折内拟合。采用Ridge回归对均值池化嵌入预测留出程序的得分，与匹配置换零分布对比；同时与组织组成、可解释细胞计数特征、图与度量解码器等竞争模型比较，并进行Riemannian-minus-Euclidean分解以定位信号来源。

Result: 嵌入预测留出程序得分Spearman rho=0.25-0.56，UNI2在所有四个程序上最强（免疫0.556）；置换检验p~1e-4，Holm校正p=0.0044。嵌入优于组织组成（ER/腔面+0.280，增殖+0.284，免疫+0.479，p<=0.003），但基底程序无显著差异（0.493 vs 0.469，p=0.77）。54个可解释细胞计数特征在每个程序上差距仅0.043-0.085。几何机制贡献可忽略（Riemannian minus Euclidean=+0.0010，CI[-0.0007,+0.0029]），Ridge回归比图与度量解码器好+0.097；91.8%的随机六基因面板恢复>=5/6驱动基因。

Conclusion: 病理基础模型的组织形态学分子信号是真实且可跨患者预测的，但该信号本质上来自嵌入表示本身而非文献中常强调的几何/拓扑机制——测地线图因欧氏最近邻选边本质上仍是欧氏拓扑。结果提示该领域的信号源应被重新归因，简单Ridge回归与可解释细胞计数特征已能达到相当性能，复杂的图与度量解码器及驱动计数指标在此场景下收益有限。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are reported to encode molecular programmes in tissue morphology, but the evidence is usually a cohort-wide ranked gene list rather than a prediction for a held-out patient. We rebuild such an analysis with the patient as the unit of evidence and ask which pipeline component carries signal.
  Across 11 frozen backbones, four pre-specified gene programmes and 285 TCGA-BRCA patients with paired slides and RNA-seq (44 cells; GroupKFold by patient, all preprocessing fitted inside the fold), ridge regression on mean-pooled embeddings predicts held-out programme scores at Spearman rho = 0.25-0.56, UNI2 strongest on all four (immune 0.556). A matched permutation null gives raw p ~ 1e-4 at 10,000 permutations for every cell; Holm-adjusted p = 0.0044.
  The signal is real but not uniformly morphological. Against competing models on the same patients and folds, embeddings beat tissue composition for ER/luminal, proliferation and immune (+0.280, +0.284, +0.479; p <= 0.003) but not basal, where compartment fractions alone reach 0.469 against the embedding's 0.493 (p = 0.77). Fifty-four interpretable cell-count features come within 0.043-0.085 on every programme.
  The geometric machinery contributes nothing measurable, and we identify why: the geodesic graph selects neighbours by Euclidean nearest-neighbour search and only reweights edges already chosen, so the topology is Euclidean by construction (Riemannian minus Euclidean = +0.0010, 95% CI [-0.0007, +0.0029]). Applied consistently the geometry is worse (-0.0117). Ridge regression beats the graph-and-metric decoder by +0.097 (CI [+0.069, +0.127]). The driver-count metric common in this literature is near-uninformative here: 91.8% of random six-gene panels recover >=5/6 drivers.

</details>


### [27] [Distill What RGB Can Recover: Privileged 3D Evidence for RGB-Only Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00110)
*Yanbin Hu,Jin Cui,Jun Ye,Jiepeng Zhou,Jiangcheng Song,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种特权证据蒸馏框架，通过在训练时利用3D空间证据（如深度或3D位置信息）提升模型的空间推理能力，但推理时仅使用RGB图像和问题，无需额外的推理时输入。通过统一证据接口、受控残差注入和证据敏感性引导蒸馏，取得优于基线及多个基准的最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景理解需要推理实体存在、空间布局和对象关系，但仅RGB图像通常缺乏足够的3D线索。现有3D-VLM在推理时依赖深度或3D位置感知输入，带来额外的采集、重建或标注成本，限制了纯RGB部署。

Method: 提出特权证据蒸馏框架：(1) 通过统一证据接口和受控残差注入构建可蒸馏的教师模型；(2) 通过logit蒸馏和结构化表示蒸馏将知识转移给仅接收RGB图像和问题的学生模型；(3) 引入证据敏感性引导蒸馏，利用损坏的证据识别高度依赖证据的目标并降低其监督权重；(4) 定义可恢复性分解，基于匹配的基线、教师和学生，将特权增益分为RGB可恢复改进和残余教师优势。

Result: 在四个基准上，教师模型在11项指标中7项取得最佳结果；纯RGB学生模型在所有11项指标上优于其匹配基线，包括ScanQA CIDEr提升10.4、Scan2Cap CIDEr@0.5提升19.1，且无需额外推理时输入。

Conclusion: 训练时的特权3D证据蒸馏对教师性能和可部署的纯RGB空间推理均有效；匹配的基线-教师-学生分析揭示了不同证据类型和空间技能上的特权增益转移特性。

Abstract: 3D scene understanding requires reasoning about entity existence, spatial layout, and object relations, yet RGB images alone often provide insufficient 3D cues. Existing 3D-VLMs commonly rely on depth or 3D-position-aware inputs at inference time, introducing additional acquisition, reconstruction, or annotation costs that limit RGB-only deployment. We therefore study how training-time 3D evidence can be converted into spatial reasoning capabilities retained under RGB-only inference. We propose a privileged-evidence distillation framework that constructs a distillable teacher through a unified evidence interface and controlled residual injection, and transfers its knowledge to a deployable student receiving only RGB images and questions through logit and structured representation distillation. To avoid imitating teacher signals unsupported by RGB, we further introduce evidence-sensitivity-guided distillation, which uses corrupted evidence to identify highly evidence-dependent targets and down-weight their supervision. We also define a recoverability decomposition based on the matched baseline, teacher, and student, separating privileged gains into RGB-recoverable improvements and residual teacher advantages. Across four benchmarks, the teacher achieves the best result on 7 of 11 reported metrics among the compared methods. The RGB-only student outperforms its matched baseline on all 11 metrics, including gains of 10.4 ScanQA CIDEr and 19.1 Scan2Cap CIDEr@0.5, without additional inference-time inputs. These results validate the effectiveness of training-time privileged 3D evidence distillation for both teacher performance and deployable RGB-only spatial reasoning. Separately, our matched baseline-teacher-student analysis characterizes privileged-gain transfer across evidence types and spatial skills.

Method: 将受损建筑映射重构为在存档的预打击地图上的零样本几何投影任务。具体包括：(1) 从LiveUAMap和ArcGIS的坐标及事件文本中，利用大型语言模型（LLMs）提取武器载荷W；(2) 通过Hopkinson-Cranz缩放定律（R_base = Z * W^(1/3)）投影动能爆炸周长；(3) 引入两项技术创新——自适应视场（Adaptive Field-of-View）消除SAMGeo 2D分割中的分辨率（缩放）偏差；(4) 构建2.5D伪高度深度图与分割掩膜结合，帮助大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）解析重叠密集的屋顶结构。

Result: 在2026年中东冲突数据上评估，深度增强的LVLMs在拥挤的城市中心区域显著优于传统分割方法。该混合范式在零样本危机测绘中展现出高效性能：超快速2D分割适用于稀疏农村区域，而深度增强LVLMs则在密集城市环境中表现更佳。

Abstract: Rapid estimation of impacted structures - critical for conflict-zone humanitarian response - is frequently hindered by post-strike satellite data embargoes and imagery blackouts. We bypass this operational bottleneck by reframing impacted building mapping as a zero-shot geometric projection task on archival, pre-strike maps. Using coordinate and incident text from LiveUAMap and ArcGIS, Large Language Models extract weapon payloads (W) to project kinetic blast perimeters via Hopkinson-Cranz scaling (R_base = Z * W^(1/3)). To count exposed structures within these zones without post-strike imagery, we introduce two technical innovations: Adaptive Field-of-View to eliminate resolution (zoom) bias in 2D segmentation (SAMGeo), and 2.5D pseudo-height depth maps combined with segmentation masks to help Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) resolve overlapping, dense rooftops. Evaluated on 2026 Middle East conflict data, depth-augmented LVLMs dramatically outperform traditional segmentation in congested urban centers. This establishes a powerful hybrid paradigm for zero-shot crisis mapping: ultra-fast 2D segmentation for sparse rural zones, and depth-augmented LVLMs for dense urban environments.

TL;DR: RadPRISM是一个基于临床定义放射学模式进行概念分层对齐的视觉-语言预训练框架，将去中心化LLM提取的概念文本跨度与专用视觉子空间对齐，在胸片零样本分类、视觉定位和概念分层检索上均超越全局对齐基线及CARZero参考模型。

  <summary>Details</summary>
